\begin{abstract}
\DIFaddbegin 

\begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{精简}{压缩约 30 词；把“两种失败”并入同一句；去掉摘要里未定义的 clean-answer AUROC；“the procedure owns”改为被动（own 的主语统一为 checkpoint 或 cell）。}\end{DIFnomarkup}
\DIFaddend A linear probe reads a clinical finding from the visual representation that a vision-language model's language model consumes\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Activation }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and activation }\DIFaddend steering suggests the converse: writing the probe direction back \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{into that representation }\DIFdelend should move the model's answer about the finding, making the direction a handle on the concept. Evaluations \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{score }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{test }\DIFaddend whether models encode findings and answer questions about them, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{but }\DIFdelend not whether such a handle exists. We test it with \emph{ownership}: at a fixed dose on the same patients, does a concept's own direction move its own answer more than the directions fitted for every competing finding? Across \cfCheckpoints{} checkpoints on NIH ChestX-ray14, CheXpert Plus and COCO, clinical concepts are readable in \cfChestReadablePct{}\% of chest checkpoint--concept cells and answerable in \cfChestAnswerablePct{}\%, but owned in \cfChestOwnedPct{}\%\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Two failures combine}\DIFdelend : most clinical writes do not move their own answer beyond random directions of the same dose, and the same directions move the other findings' answers. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The procedureowns }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Under the same procedure, }\DIFaddend \cfCocoOwnedPct{}\% of natural-image object cells \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{are owned}\DIFaddend , and the gap persists among cells the model answers well (\cfStratChestTopPct{}\% against \cfStratNatTopPct{}\%\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{at a clean-answer AUROC of at least }%DIFDELCMD < \cfStratTopEdge{}%%%
\DIFdelend ). Non-clinical attributes of the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{same radiographs are owned no more often than findings}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{radiographs share the deficit}\DIFaddend , and checkpoints that share a vision tower, and therefore write identical vectors, own different concepts\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. The }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{: the }\DIFaddend deficit follows the chest-radiograph representation and the model that reads it\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{rather than the concept's clinical status}\DIFdelend . A direction fitted to the model's own answers provides the handle that the label direction lacks. A probe's readability does not confer control through its direction.
\end{abstract}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{全局}{本版包含两轮修改：第二轮（写作、公式、术语、退回另一个 agent 的部分改动）和第三轮（按主线精简，细节移入附录 A.10 Further results）。每处批注只说明原因；具体增删看红蓝标记。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \section{Introduction}
\label{sec:introduction}

Linear probes are the standard tool for testing what a vision-language model (VLM) represents. They read out a concept by fitting a classifier to activations at a chosen layer \citep{alain2017understanding,belinkov2022probing}. In medical imaging, probes often succeed at linearly decoding clinical findings such as pleural effusion or cardiomegaly from the visual representation of general-purpose and medical VLMs \citep{zhu2026lost,pedersen2026probes}. Activation steering promises the converse: writing the probe direction back into the representation to change the model's behaviour \citep{turner2023steering,panickssery2024caa}. Together, these methods \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{promise to make }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{cast }\DIFaddend the probe direction \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{as }\DIFaddend a handle on the concept: read it to detect, write it to control. We ask whether that handle exists.

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{写作}{0.190 补上单位（$P(\mathrm{yes})$ 的平均变化）；“readable but not owned”的主语改为概念，因为 readable 是概念的属性而不是方向的属性。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend A steering write that changes the answer does not settle the question. In Qwen2.5-VL-7B on NIH ChestX-ray14, a probe reads effusion from the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{consumed visual tokens }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{visual tokens the language model consumes}\DIFaddend , and writing its direction back raises the model's \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Effusion answer }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{probability of answering yes to the Effusion question }\DIFaddend by $\cfSeedWEff$, more than the 95th percentile of 119 random directions and a coordinate-permutation sham written at the same dose. By the usual steering criteria, the write works. Writing the Nodule direction into the same patients at the same dose raises the Effusion answer further, by $\cfSeedWNodule$ (Figure~\ref{fig:framework}c), although Nodule is the competing direction least similar to Effusion (Appendix~\ref{app:cf-robustness}). Random and sham directions carry no label, so they cannot expose a direction whose effect is shared with other findings \citep{pahde2025navigating}. Exposing it requires writing every competing clinical direction at the same locus and dose in the same patients and asking whether the concept's own direction moves the concept's own answer more than any other. We call the outcome \emph{ownership}. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{A probe direction that }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{When a concept }\DIFaddend is readable but not owned\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{reads its }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, its probe direction reads the }\DIFaddend concept without writing it: reading is not writing.

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{术语}{在这里首次命名 consumed block（原文 primary locus、consumed locus、final visual block 等五种叫法并存）；clean 在此处尚未定义，改为 without any write。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend We turn this comparison into an evaluation (Section~\ref{sec:framework}, Figure~\ref{fig:framework}). At the \DIFaddbegin \emph{\DIFadd{consumed block}}\DIFadd{, the }\DIFaddend final visual block whose output the language model \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{consumes}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{reads}\DIFaddend , each concept receives three grades. It is \emph{readable} if its probe beats random-label probes of equal capacity, \emph{answerable} if the model's \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{clean }\DIFdelend yes/no answer \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{without any write }\DIFaddend beats chance, and \emph{owned} if a fixed-dose write along its direction clears the random and sham references and moves its own answer more than every competing clinical direction under simultaneous bounds. We apply the evaluation to \cfCheckpoints{} checkpoints from \cfFamiliesWord{} families (3B--72B; general-purpose and medical) on two chest-radiograph datasets with report-derived labels, NIH ChestX-ray14 and CheXpert Plus, and on COCO objects as a natural-image control (Section~\ref{sec:setup}).

\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The three grades separate on chest radiographs (Section~\ref{sec:results}, Table~\ref{tab:cf-main}, Figure~\ref{fig:overview}). Clinical concepts are readable in }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestReadablePct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of chest cells and answerable in }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestAnswerablePct%%%
\DIFdel{\%, but ownedin }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestOwnedPct%%%
\DIFdel{\%, and the seed cell reproduces on new patients. The deficit has two parts. Most clinical writes do not move their own answer beyond the random and sham references}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{第四轮·精简}{原第 4、5 段合并为一段。59\%/66\%/13\%、COCO 75\%、AUROC$\geq$0.90 分层这些数字摘要里已经给过，引言不再重复，只保留结论；摘要里没有的 answer direction 计数（19/36 对 9/36）保留。在此命名 attribution deficit，此后全文只用这个名字。“readable/answerable/owned in X\% of cells”改为以概念作主语，cell 在引言里第一次出现时写全 checkpoint--concept cell。clean answer 在引言里还没定义，改为 the model's own answers（与摘要措辞一致）。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFadd{On chest radiographs most readable and answerable concepts are not owned}\DIFaddend , \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{while most COCO objects are, }\DIFaddend and the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{effect the writes do have spreads across findings: the written concept's own question receives }%DIFDELCMD < \cfOwnShareNih%%%
\DIFdel{\% of the positive answer change on NIH, against }%DIFDELCMD < \cfOwnShareCoco%%%
\DIFdel{\% on COCO. The write itself is intact. The same procedure owns }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCocoOwnedPct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of COCO cells, six small or fine-grained object categories are owned at the rate of the easy ones, and among cells with a clean-answer AUROC of at least }%DIFDELCMD < \cfStratTopEdge{}%%%
\DIFdel{, }%DIFDELCMD < \cfStratChestTopPct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of chest cells are owned against }%DIFDELCMD < \cfStratNatTopPct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of natural-image cells.
}%DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\DIFdel{Three further }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{gap persists among concepts the model answers well (Section~\ref{sec:results}). We call this gap the }\emph{\DIFadd{attribution deficit}}\DIFadd{. Since the same write succeeds on natural images, the remaining }\DIFaddend results locate the deficit. Checkpoints that share a vision tower write identical vectors into identical representations \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, }\DIFdelend yet own different concepts, so the model that reads the representation decides ownership\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, not the representation alone}\DIFdelend . Non-clinical attributes of the same radiographs (projection, sex\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend and age) are owned no more often than findings, so the deficit \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{follows the chest-radiograph representation and its reader rather than the concept 's clinicalstatus}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{does not depend on the concept being clinical}\DIFaddend . The reader responds to a different direction: a direction fitted to the model's own \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{clean answer margin }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{answers }\DIFaddend is owned in \cfAnsChestOwnedA{} of \cfAnsChestN{} chest \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{checkpoint--concept }\DIFaddend cells where the label direction is owned in \cfAnsChestOwnedLabel{}, and on chest radiographs it is nearly orthogonal to the probe direction.

\begin{figure}[t]
    \centering
    \resizebox{\textwidth}{!}{\includegraphics{figures/fig1_method.pdf}}
    \caption{\textbf{Reading and writing a concept at the consumed block.} (a) The probe reads the concept from the consumed visual tokens\DIFdelbeginFL \DIFdelFL{; }\DIFdelendFL \DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{, and }\DIFaddendFL its lifted normal is written back to them at \DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{dose }\DIFaddendFL $\alpha=+0.25$\DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{, a quarter of each token's norm}\DIFaddendFL . (b) The write matrix $W_{q,d}$\DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{, the mean change in $P(\mathrm{yes})$ of question $q$ under a write along direction $d$, }\DIFaddendFL and the three comparisons that decide ownership. (c) The \DIFdelbeginFL \DIFdelFL{seed cell, }\DIFdelendFL Effusion \DIFdelbeginFL \DIFdelFL{in }\DIFdelendFL \DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{case (}\DIFaddendFL Qwen2.5-VL-7B\DIFdelbeginFL \DIFdelFL{on }\DIFdelendFL \DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{, }\DIFaddendFL NIH ChestX-ray14\DIFdelbeginFL \DIFdelFL{, whose verdict is }\DIFdelendFL \DIFaddbeginFL \DIFaddFL{): a }\DIFaddendFL stronger competitor.}
    \label{fig:framework}
\end{figure}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{第四轮·精简}{每条贡献是一句完整的 We… 句子，说明做了什么、与已有做法的区别、得出什么结论（与 CoverPruner 的写法一致）；不再复述第 3 段已经交代的网格（25 个 checkpoint、12 个家族、三个数据集）。(ii) 仍不写 where both models answer well（比较的是同一批模型在两个数据集上，both models 不成立），改为 at matched answer ability。(iii) 把原来三个分句的罗列（换塔、属性、answer direction）压成“读法而非临床身份”加 answer direction 两点。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \paragraph{Contributions.} (i) We \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{separate }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{introduce ownership, which separates }\DIFaddend reading a concept from writing it\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{and make the difference measurable. Ownership scores }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{: }\DIFaddend a probe direction \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{is scored }\DIFaddend against directions fitted the same way for the competing concepts, written at the same locus and dose in the same patients \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, alongside readability and answerability at the consumed visual block }\DIFdelend (Section~\ref{sec:framework}). (ii) We \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{measure the three grades across }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCheckpoints{} %%%
\DIFdel{checkpoints, }%DIFDELCMD < \cfFamiliesWord{} %%%
\DIFdel{families, three datasets and }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{identify the attribution deficit: across }\DIFaddend \cfCells{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{concept cells. Clinical concepts }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{checkpoint--concept cells, clinical concepts on chest radiographs }\DIFaddend are read and answered far more often than they are owned, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{while the chest-to-natural-image ownership gap persists where both models answer well }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{and the gap to natural-image objects persists at matched answer ability }\DIFaddend (Sections~\ref{sec:headline}--\ref{sec:coco}). (iii) We \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{locate the deficit in }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{trace the deficit to }\DIFaddend how the model reads the chest-radiograph representation \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{: identical writes are owned differently by different language models, non-clinical attributes of the radiograph share the deficit, and }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{rather than to the concept's clinical status, and show that a direction fitted to }\DIFaddend the model's own \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{answer direction }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{answers }\DIFaddend provides the handle that the label direction lacks (Sections~\ref{sec:reader}--\ref{sec:ansdir}). We release the evaluation, scorecards\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend and write matrices; a new model needs one adapter naming its consumed block.
\DIFaddbegin 

\DIFaddend \section{Background and Related Work}
\label{sec:related}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{精简}{合并短句；erasure 与本文问题的对照压成一句；两篇医学 probing 工作合为一句，结尾改为正面定位而非 Neither…。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \paragraph{Probing and its controls.}
Linear \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{classifiers have long been used to }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{probes }\DIFaddend track information across network depth \citep{alain2017understanding}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. A }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and a }\DIFaddend probe score reflects both the representation and the learner fitted to it\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. This }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, which }\DIFaddend motivates matched-capacity probes and control tasks \citep{hewitt2019designing}. The probing literature distinguishes information a readout can recover from information the model uses \citep{belinkov2022probing,ravichander2021probing}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Causal erasure pursues the complementary test \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{ravfogel2020null,elazar2021amnesic}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, and a closed-form projection removes a concept from }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; causal erasure tests use by deleting a concept \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{ravfogel2020null,elazar2021amnesic}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, in closed form for }\DIFaddend every linear reader at once \citep{belrose2023leace}. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Erasure asks what breaks when a concept is deleted; the question here is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Our readability grade adopts the control-task idea, and the write test asks a question complementary to erasure: }\DIFaddend whether the concept's own direction is the one that moves its answer. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Our readability grade adopts the control-task idea: it measures selectivity against random-label probes of equal capacity fitted within recurring image types. Depth-wise probing of }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{In }\DIFaddend medical multimodal models\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{has mapped }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, depth-wise probing maps }\DIFaddend classification information across towers, connectors, and language layers \citep{zhu2026lost}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Layer-wise }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and layer-wise }\DIFaddend probes of MedCLIP expose acquisition and device shortcuts \citep{pedersen2026probes}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Both map what can be read; the write test here intervenes }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; we intervene }\DIFaddend on the decoded clinical direction at the locus where it is read.

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{写作}{原 CAV 段与 steering 段合并；原来一句一引、三处“ownership 不同于…”的防御式定位，合成一句定位；cosine 度量约定移到附录；所有引用保留。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \paragraph{Concept directions and \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{what they inherit}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{steering}\DIFaddend .}
A concept activation vector is the normal of a classifier separating concept examples from a negative set, and it grades a class by the directional derivative of the logit along that normal \citep{kim2018tcav}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. We keep the direction and change the measurement: a bounded forward write at a fixed fraction of each token's norm, scored against directions built the same way for the competing concepts, rather than a gradient sensitivity read at the output}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; we write the direction forward instead}\DIFaddend . Such a normal shifts with the layer it is fitted at, entangles with correlated concepts, depends on \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the negative setit is fitted against}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{its negative set}\DIFaddend , and carries where in the image the concept was read \citep{nicolson2025explaining}. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Section~\ref{sec:robust-geometry} tests each: alternative constructions, a second locus, and writes concentrated on the tokens the probe reads.
}%DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\paragraph{\DIFdel{Steering with probe normals.}}
%DIFAUXCMD
\addtocounter{paragraph}{-1}%DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{Representation engineering makes the step from reading a concept to writing it back \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{zou2023representation}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. Activation engineering changes }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Representation engineering and activation steering write a concept back by changing }\DIFaddend intermediate representations at inference time \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{and measures the resulting output changes \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{turner2023steering,panickssery2024caa}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. A }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{zou2023representation,turner2023steering,panickssery2024caa}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, and a }\DIFaddend probe normal is an appealing intervention because it supplies a label and a signed vector from the same representation. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Separability-trained concept vectors can}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{A separability-trained vector can, however, }\DIFaddend absorb distractor signals and diverge from \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the concept they are meant to model \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{pahde2025navigating}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. A }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{its concept \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{pahde2025navigating}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
: a }\DIFaddend classifier's \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{weight vector is a filter , not the pattern it detects \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{haufe2014interpretation}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. Mass-mean }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{weights are a filter rather than the pattern they detect \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{haufe2014interpretation}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, mass-mean }\DIFaddend directions steer truth judgements \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{in language models more effectively }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{better }\DIFaddend than logistic normals \citep{marks2024geometry}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. The }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and the }\DIFaddend features that identify a concept \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{in the input }\DIFdelend are largely not \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the features }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{those }\DIFaddend that control the output \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, and selecting for output effect improves steering }\DIFdelend \citep{arad2025saes}. Benchmarks accordingly score concept detection and steering as separate axes \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{of the same method \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{wu2025axbench}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. Random-direction and sham controls test generic sensitivity and coordinate dependence. They do not test whether an equally constructed direction with a different label would move the same answer more. Ownership adds this comparison and evaluates it in the forward path at the consumed locus, using each token's norm as the dose scale. Alignment }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{wu2025axbench}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, and alignment }\DIFaddend search certifies a subspace as causal \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{by showing that }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{when }\DIFaddend it implements an interpretable \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{causal variable \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{geiger2024alignments}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
; ownership asks a narrower question of a labelled direction: whether the label names the direction that moves its answer. Prior work replaces the separability-trained vector with a better estimator of the same concept and evaluates the replacement against the concept's own label \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{pahde2025navigating,haufe2014interpretation,marks2024geometry}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. Ownership instead }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{variable \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{geiger2024alignments}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. These works judge a direction mainly against its own concept. Ownership }\DIFaddend fixes the estimator and varies the label \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{: the reference family contains }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{instead: it asks whether }\DIFaddend a direction fitted the same way for \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{every other concept in the set, so a construction that improves estimation is scored by whether it wins that comparison}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{another concept moves the same answer more}\DIFaddend . Section~\ref{sec:robust-geometry} runs \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{five constructionsand both lifts through the same }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{alternative constructions, a second locus, and token-concentrated writes through this }\DIFaddend test.

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{写作}{this paper 改为 we（避免第三人称叙述）；删去末句 which is the comparison that decides… 这类自我辩护。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \paragraph{Writing into a vision-language model.}
Image features are linearly decodable in a vision-language model's hidden layers, and targeted edits to them change downstream answers \citep{rajaram2025lineofsight}. Steering one sparse-autoencoder feature of the vision tower \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{on an empty image }\DIFdelend and asking the language model what it sees \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{turns the language model into a read-out of }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{recovers }\DIFaddend what that feature encodes \citep{ferrando2026explain}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. That work steers a feature to recover its meaning; this paper starts }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; we start }\DIFaddend from the meaning, fit\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{s}\DIFdelend  a direction to a clinical label, and ask\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{s}\DIFdelend  whether the write keeps that label's selectivity \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{when }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{against }\DIFaddend the other findings' directions\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{are written at the same locus, dose, and patients}\DIFdelend . Causal probing of multimodal models intervenes on the language model's residual stream with difference-in-means vectors \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{over paired images, and reads out where entity and abstract concepts sit across depth \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{deng2026causal}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. It intervenes inside the reader and }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{and }\DIFaddend scores each concept against its own effect \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{; the write here acts on }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{deng2026causal}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
; we write }\DIFaddend the representation the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{reader consumes and scores }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{language model consumes and score }\DIFaddend each concept against \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the competing concepts' directions, which is the comparison that decides whether the label names the handle.
}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{its competitors.
}

\DIFaddend \section{Reading, Answering, and Writing at \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{a }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{the }\DIFaddend Consumed \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Locus}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Block}\DIFaddend }
\label{sec:framework}

We ask three questions about a concept $c$ in a checkpoint $M$ on a dataset with binary labels: whether $c$ is readable from the visual representation the language model consumes, whether $M$ answers the question about $c$, and whether writing $c$'s own direction moves $c$'s answer more than any alternative. A registered protocol fixes an estimator and a decision rule for each question (Appendix~\ref{app:cf-labels}).

\subsection{Consumed \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{locus }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{block }\DIFaddend and matched read--write positions}
\label{sec:locus}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{公式}{标题与正文统一为 consumed block；在 Eq.~1 之前说明 $\hat\wvec_c$ 是什么、定义 $D$（原先先用后定义）；补一句 $\alpha$ 的符号含义（负剂量写向负极）。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend Every VLM in the grid passes an image through a vision tower and hands a specific tensor to a connector or merger that produces the language model's inputs. The \emph{\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{primary locus}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{consumed block}\DIFaddend } is the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{output of the }\DIFdelend last visual block whose output that consumer reads, restricted to the token positions the consumer actually uses (CLS or padding rows are excluded where the consumer drops them); the \emph{connector locus} is the consumer's output. Appendix~\ref{app:repro-loci} identifies the block for every family. For image $i$ and consumed token $t$\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend let $\hvec_{it}\in\R^D$ be the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{block output }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{output of the consumed block, where $D$ is the tower's hidden width}\DIFaddend ; mean pooling over the consumed tokens gives the read vector $\hvec_i$. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The write acts on the }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{A write adds the unit direction $\hat\wvec_c$ of concept $c$ (Section~\ref{sec:directions}) to the }\DIFaddend same tokens,
\begin{equation}
\hvec'_{it}=\hvec_{it}+\alpha\,\lVert\hvec_{it}\rVert_2\,\hat\wvec_c,
\label{eq:intervention}
\end{equation}
so that the dose $\alpha$ is a fraction of each token's own norm\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{and is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend comparable across families whose activation scales differ by orders of magnitude\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and its sign selects the concept's positive or negative pole}\DIFaddend . Reading and writing therefore use the same representation and the same positions. The connector and the language model, everything downstream of the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{primary locus}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{consumed block}\DIFaddend , form the \emph{reader} of that representation.

\subsection{Directions, controls, and the reference family}
\label{sec:directions}

\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{A fixed seed-0 }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{公式}{原 Eq.~2 分子分母重复同一长式、$\oslash$ 未定义，改为 $\boldsymbol g_c$ 形式，并解释为 probe logit 对 block 输出的梯度；$C=1$ 改为文字，避免与概念 $c$ 混淆；在此命名 label direction，与后文 answer direction 对照。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFadd{Each concept is read by a linear probe on a shared 512-dimensional view of the representation. A fixed }\DIFaddend Gaussian matrix $R\in\R^{D\times512}$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{projects every checkpoint to a common readout dimension; featurewise scale }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{maps the read vector to features $\boldsymbol x_i=R^{\top}\hvec_i$, which are standardised with training-row means and scales }\DIFaddend $\boldsymbol s$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{is fitted on training rows only; logistic regression ($C=1$}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; an $\ell_2$-regularised logistic regression with coefficient vector $\boldsymbol\beta_c$ then scores concept $c$ (inverse regularisation strength 1}\DIFaddend , 2{,}000 iterations\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{) is fitted per concept with three seeds. The write direction lifts the positive-class coefficient $\boldsymbol\beta_c$ back to the consumed space, }%DIFDELCMD < \begin{equation}
%DIFDELCMD < \hat\wvec_c=\frac{R\left(\boldsymbol\beta_c\oslash\max(\boldsymbol s,10^{-8})\right)}{\left\lVert R\left(\boldsymbol\beta_c\oslash\max(\boldsymbol s,10^{-8})\right)\right\rVert_2}.
%DIFDELCMD < \label{eq:lift}
%DIFDELCMD < \end{equation}%%%
\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, three fit seeds). The probe's logit is thus an affine function of $\hvec_i$ whose gradient is $R\,\mathrm{diag}(\boldsymbol s)^{-1}\boldsymbol\beta_c$, and the write direction is that gradient at unit length,
}\begin{equation}
\hat\wvec_c=\frac{\boldsymbol g_c}{\lVert\boldsymbol g_c\rVert_2},\qquad \boldsymbol g_c=R\,\mathrm{diag}(\boldsymbol s)^{-1}\boldsymbol\beta_c .
\label{eq:lift}
\end{equation}\DIFaddend 
\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{It is the direction in the consumed representation along which the probe's evidence for $c$ grows fastest (Appendix~\ref{app:repro-probe} gives the numerical details). We call it the concept's }\emph{\DIFadd{label direction}}\DIFadd{, since it is fitted to the concept's labels.
}

\begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{公式}{Eq.~3 里的 $y$、$r$、$y^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k$、$r^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k$ 原先在正文未定义，现先定义再给公式，并补一句 $S>0$ 的含义；cosine 度量约定一句移到附录 A.10（白化余弦已不在正文使用）。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend Two families of controls bracket the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{concept }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{label }\DIFaddend direction. For reading, 20 control probes are fitted to random labels drawn within recurring image types (view, sex, and age decade on chest sets; aspect and area buckets on COCO)\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend so that they have the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{same }\DIFdelend capacity and the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{same nuisance structure as }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{nuisance structure of }\DIFaddend the concept probe\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{; selectivity is
}%DIFDELCMD < \begin{equation}
%DIFDELCMD < S=\operatorname{AUROC}(y,r)-\tfrac{1}{20}\textstyle\sum_{k}\operatorname{AUROC}(y^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k,r^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k).
%DIFDELCMD < \label{eq:selectivity}
%DIFDELCMD < \end{equation}%%%
\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{. With $y$ the concept labels and $r$ the probe's scores on the calibration rows, and $y^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k$ and $r^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k$ the random labels and scores of control probe $k$, the probe's selectivity is
}\begin{equation}
S=\operatorname{AUROC}(y,r)-\tfrac{1}{20}\textstyle\sum_{k=1}^{20}\operatorname{AUROC}(y^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k,r^{\mathrm{ctrl}}_k),
\label{eq:selectivity}
\end{equation}\DIFaddend 
\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{which is positive when the probe reads its concept better than a probe of equal capacity reads an arbitrary label with the same nuisance structure. }\DIFaddend For writing, the \emph{reference family} contains 119 \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{seed-0 }\DIFdelend random unit directions, a coordinate-permutation sham of $\hat\wvec_c$ (same coordinates, scrambled arrangement), and the five \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{label }\DIFaddend directions of the other clinical concepts, all written at the same dose to the same images.
\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Cosine and projection depend on the inner product chosen for the representation \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{park2024linear}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, so each cosine names its metric: cosines between directions are reported in the raw model space, and the two comparisons that carry the argument, the answer direction against the probe normal and the expert-label refit against the report-label direction , are also reported under the training covariance. }\DIFdelend 

\subsection{Ownership}
\label{sec:ownership}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{公式}{写明问题与方向按概念统一编号（否则 $W_{q,q}$ 的含义不清）；$W_{q,d}$、$\sigma$、$\ell_{\mathrm{yes}}$ 在首次使用处定义；30 个对比写明 6$\times$5，并说明 max-$T$ 同时界的含义；seed cell 改为 Effusion case（seed 与随机种子重名）。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFadd{Each dataset has six concepts, and we index both the yes/no question about a concept and the direction fitted for it by that concept. }\DIFaddend For question $q$ and direction $d$, $W_{q,d}$ is the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{paired mean }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{mean over test images of the }\DIFaddend change in $P(\mathrm{yes})$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{of }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{for question }\DIFaddend $q$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{under a writealong $d$, where $P(\mathrm{yes})=\sigma(\ell_{\mathrm{yes}}-\ell_{\mathrm{no}})$ }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{when $\hat\wvec_d$ is written, each image scored with and without the write. Here $P(\mathrm{yes})=\sigma(\ell_{\mathrm{yes}}-\ell_{\mathrm{no}})$, where $\sigma$ is the logistic function and $\ell_{\mathrm{yes}}$ and $\ell_{\mathrm{no}}$ are the log-probabilities of the two answers }\DIFaddend at the first answer position\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{and each log-probability is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, each }\DIFaddend the maximum over case and leading-space variants that tokenise to one token. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Row $q$ of the }\DIFaddend $6\times6$ \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{write }\DIFaddend matrix $W$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{over concept questions and concept directions is the object of interest }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{records how far each direction moves question $q$, and its diagonal entry $W_{q,q}$ is the concept's own write }\DIFaddend (Figure~\ref{fig:write-matrices}). Ownership is the diagonal margin
\begin{equation}
O_q=W_{q,q}-\max_{d\neq q}W_{q,d},
\label{eq:margin}
\end{equation}
the advantage of the concept's own direction over its strongest clinical competitor on the concept's own question. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Ownership is a comparison at one fixed dose, on the same patients, against directions built the same way for the other concepts. }\DIFdelend $W_{q,q}$ measures how far the concept's own direction moves its answer; $O_q$ measures whether it moves that answer further than every \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{such competitor }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{competitor built the same way}\DIFaddend . A large $W_{q,q}$ shows that $d=q$ can write the answer; only $O_q>0$ shows that the label names the effective direction. A concept can therefore fail ownership in two ways: its own write stays within the random and sham references, or the write clears them without holding its advantage over every competitor. Section~\ref{sec:headline} measures both. The \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{seed cell }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Effusion case of the introduction }\DIFaddend illustrates the latter: the Effusion direction raises the Effusion answer by $\cfSeedWEff$, above the random 95th percentile and the sham, and the Nodule direction raises the same answer by $\cfSeedWNodule$, so $O_{\mathrm{Effusion}}=\cfSeedEffO$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(Section~\ref{sec:seed})}\DIFdelend . Uncertainty over patients \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{is quantified by }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{comes from }\DIFaddend 2{,}000 bootstrap draws of test rows\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend with the maximum \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{in Equation~\ref{eq:margin} }\DIFaddend recomputed inside every draw\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, and the }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{. The }\DIFaddend 30 contrasts $W_{q,q}-W_{q,d}$ \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(six questions, five competitors each) }\DIFaddend receive simultaneous 95\% \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{lower }\DIFdelend bounds from a centred studentised max-$T$ bootstrap\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, which hold jointly across all 30 }\DIFaddend (Appendix~\ref{app:simultaneous-bounds}).

\subsection{Graded outcomes}
\label{sec:grades}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{术语}{定义 clean answer、cell、block，以及 own 的主语规则（checkpoint own 概念，cell/方向 is owned）；删去 Every block additionally reports… 整段（label gap 全文未用，其余移至附录 A.4）；原 3.5 门控压成一句，(A)--(G) 定义移至附录 A.3。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend The readable and answerable grades are computed on the 400 calibration rows, which carry no write. The write matrix and every ownership quantity are computed on the 600 test rows, which share no patient with the calibration rows.
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*,itemsep=1pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \textbf{Readable:} the one-sided 95\% lower bound of $S$ on the calibration rows is above zero, with at least ten known positives and ten known negatives among those 400 rows.
\item \textbf{Answerable:} the \DIFaddbegin \emph{\DIFadd{clean answer}}\DIFadd{, $P(\mathrm{yes})$ with no write, separates the concept's labels: the }\DIFaddend one-sided 95\% lower bound of \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the clean-answer }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{its }\DIFaddend AUROC on the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{yes/no question, on the }\DIFdelend same calibration rows \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, }\DIFdelend is above $0.5$, under the same ten-and-ten support rule.
\item \textbf{Owned:} on the test rows, the write meets the \emph{steering reference} ($W_{q,q}>0$, above the 95th percentile of the 119 random writes, and above the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{absolute sham}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{sham's absolute effect}\DIFaddend ) and every simultaneous lower bound of $W_{q,q}-W_{q,d}$ is positive\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, the }\emph{\DIFdel{advantage}} %DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{verdict}\DIFdelend . A cell with any simultaneous upper bound below zero has a \emph{stronger competitor}; the rest are \emph{unresolved}.
\end{itemize}
A \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{checkpoint--concept pair on one dataset is a }\emph{\DIFadd{cell}}\DIFadd{, and a checkpoint--dataset pair is a }\emph{\DIFadd{block}}\DIFadd{. A cell graded owned is said to be owned, and the checkpoint is said to own the concept; an alternative direction is owned in a cell when its write meets the same rule for that concept. A }\DIFaddend block contributes to the answerable and owned counts only when its write matrix and its calibration pass both completed. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{A probe-graded block whose write matrix is ineligible or incomplete contributes readability alone.
}\DIFdelend The grades are defined for one direction construction, one \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{relative dose per module }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{primary dose }\DIFaddend with a five-point sweep, six templates, pooled consumed-token features, and \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{one primary and one connector locus; Section~\ref{sec:robust-geometry} scores four alternative direction constructions by the same decision rule, against the write matrix's own random family and sham. Table~\ref{tab:cf-ledger} lists the rows, reference, decision rule and sham of every graded analysis.
Every block additionally reports the signed dose range of $O_q$ over $\alpha\in\{-0.5,\ldots,+0.5\}$, the standard deviation of $O_q$ over the three fit seeds (seeds 1 and 2 resample the training units with replacement and refit the scaler and the six probes), ownership and the absolute write effects at the connector locus, the label gap (under the concept's own write, the mean shift of the answer margin $\ell_{\mathrm{yes}}-\ell_{\mathrm{no}}$ }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{two loci, the consumed block and the connector output. Before a block is scored, seven acceptance checks }\DIFaddend on \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{label-positive rows minus that on label-negative rows, in logits, median over concepts), and the wording (}\emph{\DIFdel{is}} %DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{versus }\emph{\DIFdel{show}}%DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{) and mapping (A-present versus B-present) contrasts of $O_q$ across six question templates. }%DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\subsection{\DIFdel{Acceptance gates}}
%DIFAUXCMD
\addtocounter{subsection}{-1}%DIFAUXCMD
%DIFDELCMD < \label{sec:gates}
%DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\DIFdel{Seven checks run on }\DIFdelend 16 held-out preflight rows \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{confirm, }\DIFaddend at both loci\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{before block scoring (Appendix~\ref{app:cf-gates}): bitwise determinism; identity at }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, that scoring is deterministic, that }\DIFaddend $\alpha=0$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{; reach; batched-versus-single agreement of the answer logits; image-free semantic mapping of every template; throughput; and fp32 recomputation of the answer logits. To pass reach, the write must change the consumer's input and answer logits without changing anything }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{changes nothing, that the write reaches the answer and changes nothing }\DIFaddend outside the consumed tokens\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. We declare batched-versus-single deviations above $0.25$ and neutralise them by scoring every condition of a question in one fixed batch composition. We mark templates that fail semantic mapping as ineligible and close them as such. If a checkpoint's yes/no template fails, we grade it using its eligible B-positive template (IB for Llama~3.2 Vision).
The checkpoint's scorecard records the template used. The block's scorecard records every declared deviation.
}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and that every template maps its answers correctly without an image (Appendix~\ref{app:cf-gates}).
}\label{sec:gates}

\DIFaddend \section{Models, Datasets, and Scale}
\label{sec:setup}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{精简}{逐一的尺寸列表改为按通用/医学两句（尺寸见 Table 5），同时修掉一处 15pt 的溢出；塔类型与引用、cohort 簿记、模板代号、规模细节移至附录；只保留后文要用的三个胸片训练塔。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \paragraph{Checkpoints.}
We evaluate \cfCheckpoints{} instruction-tuned checkpoints \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{across }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{from }\DIFaddend \cfFamiliesWord{} families\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, 3B to 72B }\DIFaddend (Table~\ref{tab:cf-models})\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{. Six families are general-purpose}\DIFaddend : Qwen2.5-VL \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{3B/7B/32B/72B }\DIFdelend \citep{bai2025qwen25vl}, Qwen3-VL \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{4B/8B/32B }\DIFdelend \citep{qwen2025qwen3vl}, InternVL3.5 \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{8B/14B/38B }\DIFdelend \citep{wang2025internvl35}, Gemma~3 \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{4B/12B/27B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{gemma2025gemma3}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, MedGemma 4B/27B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{sellergren2025medgemma}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, Lingshu 7B/32B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{lasa2025lingshu}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{gemma2025gemma3}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, }\DIFaddend LLaVA-1.5 \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{7B/13B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{liu2024improved}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, the released LLaVA-Med~1.5 7B checkpoint \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{li2023llavamed}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{liu2024improved}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, and }\DIFaddend Llama~3.2~Vision \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{11B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{meta2024llama32vision}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, CheXagent-2-3B and CheXagent-8B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{chen2024chexagent}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, LLaVA-Rad-7B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{zambranochaves2025llavarad}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, and HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{chen2024huatuogptvision}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. Of these, }%DIFDELCMD < \cfMedicalFamiliesWord{} %%%
\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{meta2024llama32vision}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. Six }\DIFaddend are medical fine-tunes\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(MedGemma }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{: MedGemma \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{sellergren2025medgemma}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, Lingshu \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{lasa2025lingshu}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
}\DIFaddend , \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Lingshu, }\DIFdelend LLaVA-Med\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, CheXagent, LLaVA-Rad, }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{~1.5 \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{li2023llavamed}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, CheXagent \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{chen2024chexagent}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, }\DIFaddend HuatuoGPT-Vision \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{). The rest are general-purpose. The checkpoints }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{\mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{chen2024huatuogptvision}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
, and LLaVA-Rad \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{zambranochaves2025llavarad}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. They }\DIFaddend span \cfTowerDesignsWord{} vision-tower designs and three consumer designs: token insertion after a merger, token insertion after a projector, and cross-attention. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The towers include CLIP \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{radford2021clip} }\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
in the LLaVA families, SigLIP \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{zhai2023siglip} }\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
in Gemma~3, and a SigLIP~2 design \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{tschannen2025siglip2} }\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
in Qwen3-VL; MedGemma's MedSigLIP tower is a medically tuned SigLIP released with that model \mbox{%DIFAUXCMD
\citep{sellergren2025medgemma}}\hskip0pt%DIFAUXCMD
. }\DIFdelend Three towers are trained on chest radiographs\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{: the XraySigLIP tower }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, those }\DIFaddend of CheXagent-2-3B, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the EVA-CLIP tower of }\DIFdelend CheXagent-8B, and \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the BiomedCLIP-CXR tower of }\DIFdelend LLaVA-Rad-7B \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B tunes on medical data over the same stock CLIP tower as LLaVA-1.5. Consumed-token counts range from }%DIFDELCMD < \cfTokensMin{} %%%
\DIFdel{to }%DIFDELCMD < \cfTokensMax{} %%%
\DIFdel{per image. We run each checkpoint }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(Appendix~\ref{app:cf-grid}). Every checkpoint runs }\DIFaddend in bf16 at a pinned revision with its native processor\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{settings and a fixed image resolution per family}\DIFdelend .

\paragraph{Datasets and \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{concepts}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{cohorts}\DIFaddend .}
NIH ChestX-ray14 \citep{wang2017chestxray} and CheXpert Plus \citep{chambon2024chexpertplus} provide \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{frontal }\DIFaddend chest radiographs with report-derived labels\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{; the labeler that produces the CheXpert labels , its uncertainty convention, and its radiologist-annotated validation studies come from }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, the }\DIFaddend CheXpert \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{labels coming from the CheXpert labeler }\DIFaddend \citep{irvin2019chexpert}. We evaluate six findings per dataset (NIH: Effusion, Atelectasis, Pneumothorax, Cardiomegaly, Mass, Nodule; CheXpert: Effusion, Atelectasis, Pneumothorax, Cardiomegaly, Consolidation, Edema). COCO \citep{lin2014coco} provides the natural-image control, with binary presence labels for six object categories (person, dog, car, chair, bottle, bicycle). Seeded hashes of patient \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(or image ) }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{or image }\DIFaddend identity select one fixed cohort per dataset\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, shared by every checkpoint}\DIFaddend : 20{,}000 training rows (\cfCohortNihTrainRows{} on NIH\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, every row of that dataset's training split) , 400 calibration rows, 600 test rows, and 16 preflight rows. We }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{) }\DIFaddend fit the probes and the answer direction\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{on training rows . We assign }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, 400 calibration rows carry the }\DIFaddend readable and answerable grades\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{on calibration rows. We apply every write to test rows . All checkpoints use the same cohorts. Every chest image is a frontal study. On CheXpert every role holds one study per patient. On NIH the calibration, test, and preflight roles hold one study per patient and the training role holds every study of each training patient: }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCohortNihTrainRows{} %%%
\DIFdel{studies from }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCohortNihTrainUnits{} %%%
\DIFdel{patients, }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCohortNihTrainMulti{} %%%
\DIFdel{of whom contribute more than one and one of whom contributes }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCohortNihTrainMax{}%%%
\DIFdel{. Each COCO role holds one image. }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, 600 test rows carry every write, and 16 preflight rows carry the acceptance checks. }\DIFaddend No patient and no image appears in two roles of a dataset (Appendix~\ref{app:repro-cohorts}).

\paragraph{Questions and \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{templates}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{scale}\DIFaddend .}
\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{We ask }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Each concept is asked as }\DIFaddend a yes/no question \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{about each concept with }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{in }\DIFaddend six templates that cross two wordings (\emph{is there} versus \emph{does the image show}) with three answer interfaces (yes/no, A-present, B-present)\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. A template is named by its wording and its interface: I or W for }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; the }\DIFaddend \emph{is}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{or }\emph{\DIFdel{show}}%DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{, then Y, A, or B for the yes}\DIFdelend /\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{no or letter mapping. We use the }\emph{\DIFdel{is}}%DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{/}\DIFdelend yes-no template \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{for }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{gives }\DIFaddend the primary grade \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. The other five provide wording and mapping contrasts. The main write }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(Appendix~\ref{app:protocol}). The primary }\DIFaddend dose is $\alpha=+0.25$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. The dose module }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and a sweep }\DIFaddend adds $\alpha\in\{-0.5,-0.25,-0.1,+0.1,+0.5\}$. \DIFdelbegin %DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\paragraph{\DIFdel{Scale.}}
%DIFAUXCMD
\addtocounter{paragraph}{-1}%DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdel{Each block pairs one checkpoint with one dataset. It scores the $6\times6$ }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Each block scores the }\DIFaddend write matrix, the sham, and \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{the }\DIFaddend 119 random directions on \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{the }\DIFaddend 600 test rows\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(457}%DIFDELCMD < {%%%
\DIFdel{,}%DIFDELCMD < }%%%
\DIFdel{200 outcomes)}\DIFdelend . The campaign \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{targets }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCoveragePlannedWord{} %%%
\DIFdel{modules per block: the write matrix, the calibration pass, the five additional templates, the dose sweep, the two probe refits, the connector locus, and the connector-locus calibration. }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCoverageBlocksAllSeven{} %%%
\DIFdel{of the }%DIFDELCMD < \cfBlocks{} %%%
\DIFdel{blocks carry all }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCoveragePlannedWord{} %%%
\DIFdel{(Appendix~\ref{app:cf-coverage}). The campaign }\DIFdelend comprises \cfBlocks{} blocks, \cfCells{} scored concept cells, about 30 million scored answers, and roughly 3{,}000 A100-hours\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Every checkpoint has core write and calibration results on all three datasets. We grade Llama~3.2~Vision 11B on its eligible B-positive template (Appendix~\ref{app:campaign}; label handling, registration, and the released artifact are in Appendix~\ref{app:cf-labels}).
}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; }\cfCoverageBlocksAllSeven{} \DIFadd{of the }\cfBlocks{} \DIFadd{blocks carry every planned analysis (Appendix~\ref{app:cf-coverage}).
}

\DIFaddend \section{Results}
\label{sec:results}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{全局}{符号统一：column selectivity $S_d\to\kappa_d$（与 probe selectivity $S$ 重名）、write-flow $P_{q,d}\to F_{q,d}$（与 $P(\mathrm{yes})$ 重名）、$W_{qq}\to W_{q,q}$。所有表格去掉字号设置，改用 \texttt{\textbackslash fitwidth}（只缩不放的 resizebox）；p\{\} 列宽按 10/7 放大以保持原观感。结果部分由 10 个小节加 3 段 robustness 合并为 6 个小节加 1 段，每节回答主线上的一个问题。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend \subsection{Reading is not writing}
\label{sec:headline}

\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Both chest datasetsfollow the same pattern }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{写作}{开头改为直接陈述结论；原本描述表格排序与着色的一句移入表 1 表注。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFadd{On both chest datasets, reading and answering far outpace writing }\DIFaddend (Table~\ref{tab:cf-main}, Figure~\ref{fig:overview}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{). Table~\ref{tab:cf-main} orders checkpoints by their clinical owned share and shades each cell by magnitude. }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{a--b). }\DIFaddend Clinical concepts are readable in \cfChestReadable{} of \cfChestReadableN{} cells (\cfChestReadablePct\%\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{): median probe selectivity over the matched random-label controls is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; median selectivity }\DIFaddend $\cfSelChestMed$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, with quartiles $\cfSelChestQOne$ and $\cfSelChestQThree$. The models also answer: }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{), and }\DIFaddend \cfChestAnswerable{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{of }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestAnswerableN{} %%%
\DIFdel{cells }\DIFdelend (\cfChestAnswerablePct\%) \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{have a clean-answer AUROC lower bound above chance. Of these answerable cells, }%DIFDELCMD < \cfReadAns{} %%%
\DIFdel{are also readable}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{are answerable}\DIFaddend . Yet only \cfChestOwned{} cells (\cfChestOwnedPct\%) are owned\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Readability and answerability do not suffice for writing: only }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and reading and answering together barely change this: }\DIFaddend \cfReadAnsOwned{} of the \cfReadAns{} readable and answerable cells are owned (\cfReadAnsOwnedPct\%), \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{compared with }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{against }\DIFaddend \cfRestOwned{} of the remaining \cfRestN{} (\cfRestOwnedPct\%).

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{写作}{采用另一个 agent 更有力的开头；把 contingency 计数解释为“自身写入弱”这一主因，196 个 stronger competitor 与“其中 187 个在参照以下”同时出现，避免 reviewer 自行重新解读；Table 2 移至附录 A.6（数字已在正文）。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend Ownership on chest radiographs breaks at the write itself. \cfChestRefBelow{} of \cfChestCellsN{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{probe }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{label }\DIFaddend directions fail the random and sham reference for their own question\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(Table~\ref{tab:cf-contingency}). Among those below-reference cells, }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and these cells carry }\DIFaddend \cfChestBelowStrong{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{have a stronger clinical competitor; they account for }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestBelowStrongPct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of all }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{of the }\DIFaddend \cfChestCompetitor{} stronger-competitor verdicts \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(}\cfChestBelowStrongPct\DIFadd{\%; Table~\ref{tab:cf-contingency})}\DIFaddend . Once an own write clears the reference, \cfChestOwned{} of \cfChestRefMet{} cells are owned\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Readable and answerable cells follow the same pattern, while on COCO}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{; on COCO, }\DIFaddend \cfCocoRefMet{} of \cfCocoOwnedN{} writes clear \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the reference }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{it }\DIFaddend and \cfCocoOwned{} are owned. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The chest deficit therefore starts before fine-grained attribution: most probe directions }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{On chest radiographs, most label directions therefore }\DIFaddend scarcely move the answers they name, while \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{other finding directions }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{the directions of other findings }\DIFaddend often move those \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{same }\DIFdelend answers more.

\subsection{\DIFdel{Effusion exposes the ownership gap on new patients}}
%DIFAUXCMD
\addtocounter{subsection}{-1}%DIFAUXCMD
\DIFdelend \label{sec:seed}\DIFdelbegin %DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\DIFdel{The seed finding }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{The Effusion case }\DIFaddend reproduces on 600 new patients (Table~\ref{tab:cf-seed}). The Effusion write raises \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the Effusion }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{its }\DIFaddend answer by $\cfSeedWEff$, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{above the random 95th percentile ($\cfSeedEffRandP$) and the sham ($\cfSeedEffSham$), and ranks }\DIFdelend second among the 120 compared effects\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Yet the Nodule direction raises the Effusion answer }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, the Nodule write raises it }\DIFaddend by $\cfSeedWNodule$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Effusion ownership is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and }\DIFaddend $O_{\mathrm{Effusion}}=\cfSeedEffO$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(95\% CI }\DIFdelend $[\cfSeedEffOLo,\cfSeedEffOHi]$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{)}\DIFdelend , matching the $\cfSeedPriorO$ $[\cfSeedPriorOLo,\cfSeedPriorOHi]$ of the two-model \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{NIH study this campaign builds on }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{pilot study that motivated the evaluation }\DIFaddend (Appendix~\ref{app:seed}). \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The prompt dependence of Mass also reproduces (Appendix~\ref{app:seed-results}). Effusion and Cardiomegaly are the most readable of the four concepts both chest sets share, with mean selectivity $\cfSelEffusion$ and $\cfSelCardiomegaly$ over }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestProbeBlocks{} %%%
\DIFdel{chest blocks. }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Across the grid, }\DIFaddend Effusion is owned in \cfEffusionOwnedBlocks{} of \cfChestBlocks{} chest blocks\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. In }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and in }\DIFaddend \cfEffusionOwnedSmall{} of these \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{blocks, }\DIFdelend $O_{\mathrm{Effusion}}\leq0.03$.

\DIFaddend \subsection{\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The attribution deficit across }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Where }\DIFaddend the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{grid}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{chest write goes}\DIFaddend }
\label{sec:deficit}

\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{On the chest sets, ownership is the rarest and least stable of the three grades. An effect floor of $0.05$ on both $W_{q,q}$ and $O_q$ retains }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestFloored{} %%%
\DIFdel{of the }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestOwned{} %%%
\DIFdel{owned chest cells (}%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestFlooredPct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of }%DIFDELCMD < \cfChestCellsN{}%%%
\DIFdel{), compared with }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCocoFloored{} %%%
\DIFdel{of the }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCocoOwned{} %%%
\DIFdel{owned COCO cells (}%DIFDELCMD < \cfCocoFlooredPct%%%
\DIFdel{\% of }%DIFDELCMD < \cfCocoOwnedN{}%%%
\DIFdel{). Ownership survives both probe refits in }%DIFDELCMD < \cfRefitGradeStableNih{} %%%
\DIFdel{of the }%DIFDELCMD < \cfRefitGradeOwnedNih{} %%%
\DIFdel{owned NIH cells, compared with }%DIFDELCMD < \cfRefitGradeStableCoco{} %%%
\DIFdel{of }%DIFDELCMD < \cfRefitGradeOwnedCoco{} %%%
\DIFdel{on COCO (Table~\ref{tab:cf-refit}). }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{精简}{原 5.3 改名 Where the chest write goes；删去与 5.1 重复的“两部分分解”框架；effect floor 与 refit 稳定性压成一句留在本节。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend Clinical writes are not selective \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{on the chest sets}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(Figure~\ref{fig:write-flow})}\DIFaddend . The written concept's own question receives \cfOwnShareNih\% of the positive answer change on NIH and \cfOwnShareChex\% on CheXpert, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{compared with }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{against }\DIFaddend \cfOwnShareCoco\% on COCO\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(Figure~\ref{fig:write-flow}). This pattern is the attribution deficit. The model makes the concept readable to a probe. The probe's direction is not a handle on the concept. Writing it back moves other findings' answers as much as the concept's own.
}%DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < \begin{figure}[t]
%DIFDELCMD <     \centering
%DIFDELCMD <     \resizebox{\textwidth}{!}{\includegraphics{figures/fig7_write_flow.pdf}}

    %DIFAUXCMD

%DIFDELCMD < 

%DIFDELCMD < %%%
\DIFdel{The pattern is visible in individual images (Figure~\ref{fig:example}). On a chest radiograph, the Effusion write raises the Effusion answer from $\cfExChestClean$ to $\cfExChestOwn$ and the Nodule write raises it further, to $\cfExChestComp$. On a natural image, the dog write moves the dog answer from $\cfExCocoClean$ to $\cfExCocoOwn$ and moves nothing else. The write matrix reveals two components of the deficit }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{. The write matrices show the same structure }\DIFaddend (Figure~\ref{fig:write-matrices}\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, Table~\ref{tab:cf-controls}). First, concept writes in the chest datasets have little effect on their own answers. The }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{). The own write is small, with }\DIFaddend median $W_{q,q}$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{of }\DIFaddend $\cfMedianWqqNih$ on NIH and $\cfMedianWqqChex$ on CheXpert \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, compared with }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{against }\DIFaddend $\cfMedianWqqCoco$ on COCO\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Second, }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and the }\DIFaddend competing directions share \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{the effect whenever a chest write moves an answer (Table~\ref{tab:cf-contingency}). Across the grid, the }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{what effect there is: the }\DIFaddend Atelectasis, Effusion, and Consolidation directions raise each other's answers by similar amounts\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. On COCO, the diagonal entry of }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and on the chest sets a row of }\DIFaddend $W$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{dominates its row. On NIH and CheXpert, the row }\DIFdelend is flat or peaks off the diagonal\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, whereas on COCO the diagonal dominates}\DIFaddend . At every Qwen2.5-VL size \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, }\DIFdelend a competing direction raises the Effusion answer more than the Effusion direction does \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{: }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(}\DIFaddend Cardiomegaly at 3B, Nodule at 7B and 32B, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{and }\DIFdelend Atelectasis at 72B\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{.
}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{). Single images show the pattern (Figure~\ref{fig:example}): on a radiograph, the Effusion write raises the Effusion answer from $\cfExChestClean$ to $\cfExChestOwn$ and the Nodule write raises it further, to $\cfExChestComp$, while on a natural image the dog write moves the dog answer from $\cfExCocoClean$ to $\cfExCocoOwn$ and nothing else. The chest cells that are owned are also fragile: an effect floor of $0.05$ on both $W_{q,q}$ and $O_q$ keeps }\cfChestFloored{} \DIFadd{of the }\cfChestOwned{} \DIFadd{owned chest cells against }\cfCocoFloored{} \DIFadd{of the }\cfCocoOwned{} \DIFadd{on COCO, and ownership survives both probe refits in }\cfRefitGradeStableNih{} \DIFadd{of }\cfRefitGradeOwnedNih{} \DIFadd{owned NIH cells against }\cfRefitGradeStableCoco{} \DIFadd{of }\cfRefitGradeOwnedCoco{} \DIFadd{on COCO (Table~\ref{tab:cf-refit}).
}\DIFaddend

\DIFaddend \subsection{The write mechanism is intact on natural objects}
\label{sec:coco}

\DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{精简}{剂量细节压缩；COCO margin 范围、剂量模块覆盖的 block 数移至附录 A.10。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFaddend On COCO, the same procedure yields the opposite result (Table~\ref{tab:cf-main}, right block). Object ownership holds in \cfCocoOwned{} of \cfCocoOwnedN{} cells (\cfCocoOwnedPct\%)\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Ownership margins span $\cfCocoMarginMin$--$\cfCocoMarginMax$ in the Qwen, Lingshu, and Gemma families. }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, }\DIFaddend \cfCocoRefMet{} cells meet the steering reference\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Only }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, only }\DIFaddend \cfCocoCompetitor{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{cells }\DIFdelend have a stronger competitor\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, and }\DIFaddend \cfCocoBlocksFourPlus{} of the \cfCocoBlocks{} checkpoints \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{with a COCO block }\DIFdelend own at least four of the six objects. \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Dose response tracks the write. The dosemodule ran on }%DIFDELCMD < \cfDoseBlocksNih{} %%%
\DIFdel{NIH and }%DIFDELCMD < \cfDoseBlocksCoco{} %%%
\DIFdel{COCOblocks. On COCO}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{Ownership also tracks the dose: on COCO}\DIFaddend , median ownership \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{across questions and blocks }\DIFdelend rises from $\cfDoseCocoLow$ at $\alpha=-0.1$ to $\cfDoseCocoPeak$ at $\alpha=+0.25$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Median ownership remains }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{and stays }\DIFaddend positive at $\alpha=+0.5$ ($\cfDoseCocoHalf$)\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. On NIH, median ownership }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{, while on NIH it }\DIFaddend stays between $\cfDoseNihMin$ and $\cfDoseNihMax$ at every dose (Figure~\ref{fig:overview}c). The write, direction construction, reference family, and decision rules thus work as intended.

\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{The natural-image result extends to smaller, fine-grained objects}\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \begin{DIFnomarkup}\cfnote{退回 agent}{恢复总起句（agent 改为 extends to fine-grained objects 后，后半段的难度分层失去统领）；细粒度物体按实际数字写 30/36 对 34/36（原先 at the rate of the easy ones 说过头）；组内差异与 selectivity 移至附录。}\end{DIFnomarkup}

\DIFadd{The contrast survives two difficulty controls}\DIFaddend . Six small or fine-grained COCO categories (handbag, book, backpack, traffic light, knife, and cell phone) \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{are }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{remain }\DIFaddend owned in \cfFgFineOwned{} of \cfFgFineCells{} cells, \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{a rate of }%DIFDELCMD < \cfFgFineRate{}%%%
\DIFdel{, }\DIFdelend against \cfFgEasySameOwned{} of \cfFgEasySameCells{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{(}%DIFDELCMD < \cfFgEasySameRate{}%%%
\DIFdel{) }\DIFdelend for the easy six in the same \cfFgSameBlocks{} checkpoints \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Within a checkpoint the two counts of six differ by $\cfFgSameDiffMin$ to $\cfFgSameDiffMax$ cells, and an exact two-sided sign test over the checkpoints gives }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(sign test }\DIFaddend $p=\cfFgSameSignP$\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Median }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{), at a median }\DIFaddend clean-answer AUROC \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{is }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{of }\DIFaddend \cfFgFineAuroc{} against \cfFgEasySameAuroc{} \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{, and median probe selectivity }%DIFDELCMD < \cfFgFineSel{} %%%
\DIFdel{against }%DIFDELCMD < \cfFgEasySameSel{} %%%
\DIFdelend (Table~\ref{tab:cf-fgobj}). \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{Most of these fine-grained cells remain owned. }\DIFdelend On the chest sets, ownership \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{stays rare even }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{also stays rare }\DIFaddend where the model already answers the question well\DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. At }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{: at }\DIFaddend a clean-answer AUROC of at least \cfStratTopEdge{}, \cfStratChestTopOwned{} of \cfStratChestTopN{} chest cells are owned (\cfStratChestTopPct\%) against \cfStratNatTopOwned{} of \cfStratNatTopN{} natural-image cells (\cfStratNatTopPct\%), a difference of $\cfStratCvnDiff$ $[\cfStratCvnLo,\cfStratCvnHi]$ \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{. Table~\ref{tab:cf-fgobj} gives the remaining bins, the per-dataset counts, and the matched estimator, and }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{(}\DIFaddend Appendix~\ref{app:cf-crossed} states the category rule, the \DIFdelbegin \DIFdel{bin edges, the matching windows, }\DIFdelend \DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{bins, }\DIFaddend and the bootstrap\DIFaddbegin \DIFadd{)}\DIFaddend .

The shaded row is the written concept, absent from the image; values at the bar tips give its two write answers, and the dashed line marks $P(\mathrm{yes})=0.5$.}}
